```text
	<head>
		<meta charset="utf-8" />
		<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1, viewport-fit=cover" />
		<!-- Warm the connections to the image hosts early — feed/LCP images are
		     third-party, so DNS+TCP+TLS would otherwise sit on the critical path
		     after HTML parse. Which host serves depends on content-hono's
		     APTOMA_PROXY_MODE (off/png/all; see image-proxy.ts) — warm both. -->
		<link rel="preconnect" href="https://smooth-storage.aptoma.no" />
		<link rel="dns-prefetch" href="https://smooth-storage.aptoma.no" />
		<link rel="preconnect" href="https://sndk-images.b-cdn.net" />
		<link rel="dns-prefetch" href="https://sndk-images.b-cdn.net" />
		<!-- Consent + ad chain, from $lib/server/third-party.ts. Up here because the
		     handshake has to FINISH before the ad stack asks for its scripts; down
		     with the tag block below it only got as far as DNS. Below the image
		     hosts on purpose: the LCP image must not queue behind ad
		     infrastructure. -->
		<link rel="preconnect" href="https://cl-eu4.k5a.io">
<link rel="preconnect" href="https://securepubads.g.doubleclick.net">
		<!-- The CMP, first script in <head> — Cookiebot's own implementation guides
		     require that position, and everything gated waits on it: the category
		     tags below and, through the IAB SDK it loads, the ad stack's TCF signal.
		     It is separate from the tag block after SvelteKit's head because only
		     ConceptX depends on window.siteTargeting being assigned first. -->
		<script id="Cookiebot" src="https://consent.cookiebot.com/uc.js" data-cbid="4ef75988-2ac2-4fa5-824d-a27f0414413e" data-framework="TCFv2.3" data-culture="da" async></script>
		<!-- Ad/analytics context — evaluated once per pageview, before any tag reads
		     it. Device comes from the same 768px breakpoint the grid layouts switch on
		     (matchMedia, not user-agent); user state from the sn_access cookie the
		     server keeps in sync per request (none/loggedin/paying). Reads our own
		     first-party cookie and touches no storage, so it needs no consent gate.
		     Ad-slot SELECTION is resolved server-side in Banner.svelte from
		     page.data.device (Bunny's CDN-MobileDevice header) + page.data.audience,
		     so it can diverge from this viewport-based device on a narrow desktop
		     window (accepted). Consumed by CMS-authored HTML blocks; verify against
		     puck_data before removing. -->
		<script>(function(){var m=document.cookie.match(/(?:^|;\s*)sn_access=([^;]*)/);var s=m?m[1]:'none';
		window.__adContext={device:window.matchMedia('(min-width: 768px)').matches?'desktop':'mobile',
		userState:s==='paying'||s==='loggedin'?s:'anonymous'};
		window.dataLayer=window.dataLayer||[];window.dataLayer.push(window.__adContext);})();</script>
```

```text
status 404, text/html, 40018 bytes, slut-URL https://www.sn.dk/sitemap_index.xml

<!doctype html>
<html lang="da" data-site="apex">
	<head>
		<meta charset="utf-8" />
		<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1, viewport-fit=cover" />
		<!-- Warm the connections to the image hosts early — feed/LCP images are
		     third-party, so DNS+TCP+TLS would otherwise sit on the critical path
		     after HTML parse. Which host serves depends on content-hono's
		     APTOMA_PROXY_MODE (off/png/all; see image-proxy.ts) — warm both. -->
		<link rel="preconnect" href="https://smooth-storage.aptoma.no" />
		<link rel="dns-prefetch" href="https://smooth-storage.aptoma.no" />
		<link rel="preconnect" href="https://sndk-images.b-cdn.net" />
		<link rel="dns-prefetch" href="https://sndk-images.b-cdn.net" />
		<!-- Consent + ad chain, from $lib/server/third-party.ts. Up here because the
		     handshake has to FINISH before the ad stack asks for its scripts; down
		     with the tag block below it only got as far as DNS. Below the image
		     hosts on purpose: the LCP image must not queue behind ad
		     infrastructure. -->
		<link rel="preconnect" href="https://cl-eu4.k5a.io">
<link rel="preconnect" href="https://securepubads.g.doubleclick.net">
		<!-- The CMP, first script in <head> — Cookiebot's own implementation guides
		     require that position, and everything gated waits on it: the category
		     tags below and, through the IAB SDK it loads, the ad stack's TCF signal.
		     It is separate from the tag block after SvelteKit's head because only
		     ConceptX depends on window.siteTargeting being assigned first. -->
		<script id="Cookiebot" src="https://consent.cookiebot.com/uc.js" data-cbid="4ef75988-2ac2-4fa5-824d-a27f0414413e" data-framework="TCFv2.3" data-culture="da" async></script>
		<!-- Ad/analytics context — evaluated once per pageview, before any tag reads
		     it. Device comes from the same 768px breakpoint the grid layouts switch on
		     (matchMedia, not user-agent); user state from the sn_access cookie the
		     server keeps in sync per request (none/loggedin/paying). Reads our own
```
